# Skills · W · part 22

799 entries, weighted-decision-matrix to werbekennzeichnung-instagram. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **weighted-decision-matrix**: Use when the user wants to choose among several options against multiple weighted criteria with a transparent, defensible score (the weighted scoring model or Pugh matrix). Calibrates the criteria, their weights, and the scoring scale first, then scores each option in parallel, one subagent per option, before computing weighted totals, ranking, and running a sensitivity check in a markdown report. Triggers include "decision matrix", "weighted scoring", "Pugh matrix", "compare options against criteria", "which option should we choose", "weighted decision model", "selection matrix". ([l4ci/latticework](https://github.com/l4ci/latticework/tree/HEAD/skills/weighted-decision-matrix) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **weighted-density-spatial-kld-testing**: Use when spatial transcriptomics data with spatial coordinates is available and you must decide whether a gene set/pathway shows spatial organization beyond chance, rather than just cell-type enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-density-spatial-kld-testing))
- **weighted-dew-exclusion-window-parameter-tuning**: Use when tuning dynamic-exclusion behaviour of a DDA method in ViMMS: full-scan mzML files of complex mixtures (e.g. serum/beer extracts) are available and t0/t1 values for WeightedDEW must be chosen by maximizing simulated coverage before instrument validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-dew-exclusion-window-parameter-tuning))
- **weighted-distribution-compound-sampling**: Use when an unfiltered network expansion would generate too many compounds to expand further, and you want stochastic, similarity-biased pruning toward a set of target compounds rather than a hard cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-distribution-compound-sampling))
- **weighted-dominating-set-optimization**: Use when you have paired expression matrices (e.g., TCGA tumor vs normal) as a differential co-expression network and need to identify key driver nodes of transcriptome programs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-dominating-set-optimization))
- **weighted-dot-product-spectrum-comparison**: Use when scoring peptide-spectrum matches (PSMs) against simulated theoretical spectra of unlabeled or SIP-labeled peptides — e.g., validating Sipros database-search outputs in stable isotope probing (SIP) proteomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-dot-product-spectrum-comparison))
- **weighted-dynamic-exclusion-parameterization**: Use when tuning or benchmarking DDA controller parameters (e.g., WeightedDEW exclusion windows t0/t1) and you need to measure fragmentation coverage of picked peaks in mzML injections of complex mixtures against TopN and optimal upper bounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-dynamic-exclusion-parameterization))
- **weighted-engagement-scoring**: Use when combining multiple engagement signals into a single composite score, ranking content by influence or value, needing to identify high-value discussions from heterogeneous metrics like votes upvote-ratio comments sentiment and recency, or when raw engagement counts are misleading due to scale differences across metrics ([aaddrick/written-voice-replication](https://github.com/aaddrick/written-voice-replication/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/weighted-engagement-scoring) · ★14)
- **weighted-f1-benchmark-comparison**: Use when benchmarking single-cell label-transfer/integration methods on query datasets with weighted F1, e.g. reproducing Fig. 6b-e pancreas comparisons (scCobra 0.95, SCALEX 0.95, TOSICA 0.94, scVI 0.92). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-f1-benchmark-comparison))
- **weighted-f1-classification-evaluation**: Use when a multi-class document-type classifier (e.g., the keyword-based classifier over OCR-extracted text) must be scored on a real-world bidding/test split, and a single sample-weighted headline metric plus per-label diagnostics is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/weighted-f1-classification-evaluation))
- **weighted-forecast**: Use when I ask what will happen, who will win, whether I should do something, or how much something will be: research it and give a weighted-outcome forecast with a verdict. ([marek-burza/shrubbery](https://github.com/marek-burza/shrubbery/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/weighted-forecast) · ★15)
- **weighted-fragment-tree-optimization**: Use when given a low-resolution MSn peak list from a miniaturized mass spectrometer and a candidate formula constraint set, and you need a maximum weighted fragment tree to annotate top fragmentation peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-fragment-tree-optimization))
- **weighted-gdp-calc**: This skill covers tasks where you must populate an existing Excel workbook with lookup formulas, derived calculations, and weighted aggregations — all without altering formatting, adding macros, or restructuring sheets. - **weighted-geometric-mean-aggregation**: Use when qPCR CT data contain two or more internal controls (e.g. RNU48, hsa-miR-16-5p) that must be combined into one normalization reference, or when the unweighted geometric mean of reference genes fails to yield stable target normalization (e.g. non-significant ΔΔCT results). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-geometric-mean-aggregation))
- **weighted-graph-probability-computation**: Use when you have a weighted graph combining a spectral network and structural-similarity edges among MetFrag candidate structures, seed nodes from spectral library matches, and need per-node walking probabilities for annotation propagation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-graph-probability-computation))
- **weighted-hotspot-mutation-result-integration**: Use when Nano2NGS-Muta has produced per-set mutation detection results from N extracted short-read sets and final target hotspot mutation calls must be integrated and checked against a complete check-hotspot.list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/weighted-hotspot-mutation-result-integration))
- **weighted-kde-spatial-relevance-testing**: Use when analyzing 10X Visium spatial transcriptomics data to test whether curated gene sets/pathways follow anatomical or high-order spatial patterns, without clustering spots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-kde-spatial-relevance-testing))
- **weighted-knn-imputation-of-as-probability-matrices**: Use when a single-cell scRNA-seq-derived AS probability matrix (per-site PSI-like values from Leafcutter junction reads) contains NA dropouts that must be filled before unsupervised cell clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-knn-imputation-of-as-probability-matrices))
- **weighted-knn-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a single-cell AS probability matrix (rows = AS sites, columns = cells) contains NA entries from dropouts and the values must be recovered before spectral clustering, or when imputation fidelity under forced dropout must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-knn-missing-value-imputation))
- **weighted-least-squares-calibration**: Use when converting MRM peak abundances to absolute concentrations for targeted metabolites: calibration standards of known concentration (e.g. 14 standards spanning 0.025–500 mol/mL) are available and regression coefficients per transition are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-least-squares-calibration))
- **weighted-least-squares-calibration-fitting**: Use when building calibration curves from targeted proteomics dilution series (e.g. Agilent 6490 MRM data in PASSEL) and comparing unweighted vs. weighted (1/x^2) linear fits on the final linear range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/weighted-least-squares-calibration-fitting))
- **weighted-least-squares-calibration-modeling**: Use when a targeted metabolomics dataset contains calibration points, QC samples and internal-standard intensities, and absolute concentrations must be back-calculated from signal intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-least-squares-calibration-modeling))
- **weighted-least-squares-variance-approximation**: Use when reconstructing or validating ANCOM-BC's bias-correction step: you have taxa-level log-scale mean differences Delta_i between groups and need the mean difference of log sampling fractions plus a variance approximation via weighted least squares. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/weighted-least-squares-variance-approximation))
- **weighted-limit**: Weighted (co)limits: J-indexed diagrams. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/weighted-limit) · ★72)
- **weighted-loss-function-optimization**: Use when when training a dual-encoder architecture (bi-encoder + cross-encoder) on NMR spectral data where independent encoding and joint pair processing produce competing or imbalanced gradient signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/weighted-loss-function-optimization))
- **weighted-marker-aggregation**: Use when a query scRNA-seq dataset in AnnData format must be scored against a ranked cell-type marker list from a reference (e.g., MASI reference data) to produce per-cell cell-type scores and labels for downstream annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-marker-aggregation))
- **weighted-matrix**: Weighted Evaluation Matrix — score options across weighted criteria to make defensible multi-criteria decisions with an explicit numerical recommendation ([LineLuLan/line-harness](https://github.com/LineLuLan/line-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/weighted-matrix) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **weighted-mean-calculation**: How to calculate weighted means in Excel using SUMPRODUCT. Use this skill when calculating the weighted average for a group (e.g., GCC Countries) by multiplying values by their corresponding weights (e.g., GDP). ([cxcscmu/SkillLearnBench](https://github.com/cxcscmu/SkillLearnBench/tree/HEAD/skills/b4-skill-creator-gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview/weighted-gdp-calculation/weighted-mean-calculation) · ★93)
- **weighted-mean-intensity-aggregation**: Use when a RAMClustR clustering run has assigned features (MS and idMS/MS) to clusters and you need to convert the feature-by-sample abundance matrix into per-cluster spectral intensities and exportable MSP spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-mean-intensity-aggregation))
- **weighted-midpoint-center-computation**: Use when raw narrow-peak BED files from ENCODE/Roadmap chromatin profiles must be converted into fixed-length (1000 bp), non-overlapping (&gt;200 bp) genomic training intervals for a Basset/DeepFun CNN input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/weighted-midpoint-center-computation))
- **weighted-mse-loss-implementation**: Use when training a neural network to regress EI mass spectra as fixed-length (1000-bin) intensity vectors and the loss must weight m/z bins consistently with the downstream cosine-similarity library-matching score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-mse-loss-implementation))
- **weighted-nearest-neighbor-embedding-multimodal-spatial**: Use when you have spot-level feature matrices from spatial transcriptomics (e.g. 10X Visium .h5ad), spatial metabolomics (MALDI-MSI peak/intensity matrices), or haCCA-integrated multi-module matrices, and want to compare cluster counts and heterogeneity across modality views. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-nearest-neighbor-embedding-multimodal-spatial))
- **weighted-nearest-neighbor-multimodal-clustering**: Use when a droplet-based single-cell experiment (CITE-seq, ASAP-seq, TEA-seq) has paired mRNA and ADT matrices and you need joint clustering to test whether a protein normalization method (e.g., dsb vs CLR) resolves a specific cell population. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-nearest-neighbor-multimodal-clustering))
- **weighted-nearest-neighbor-multimodal-integration**: Use when Visium spatial transcriptomics data has H&E image features (e.g. VGG16/ImageNet-derived) extracted and per-modality clusters miss fine substructures, and you need a non-spatial modality integration to identify components like glomeruli or arterial spots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/weighted-nearest-neighbor-multimodal-integration))
- **weighted-nearest-neighbour-graph-construction**: Use when a MuData object with per-modality representations (e.g. PCA on RNA/ATAC PCs or MOFA factors in mdata.obsm['X_mofa']) is available and a joint cell-cell neighbour graph or UMAP combining modalities is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/weighted-nearest-neighbour-graph-construction))
- **weighted-network-aggregation-thresholding**: Use when a NeuronChat run has produced per-interaction communication networks (x@net) and you need one aggregated cell-group connectivity matrix, e.g. to benchmark predicted connectivity against retrograde-tracing labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-network-aggregation-thresholding))
- **weighted-network-edge-percentile-thresholding**: Use when you have edge-weighted undirected co-expression networks (e.g. Euclidean distance or Pearson correlation based) that are too dense for downstream fusion or community detection and need data-driven edge pruning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-network-edge-percentile-thresholding))
- **weighted-nonnegative-least-squares-deconvolution**: Use when estimating cell type proportions in bulk RNA-seq from a multi-subject scRNA-seq reference, especially when closely related cell types cause collinearity and methods like CIBERSORT or BSEQ-sc lack power to resolve them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-nonnegative-least-squares-deconvolution))
- **weighted-overlap-colocalization-measure**: Use when testing association between transcriptome region sets where the query features lack clear isoform association (e.g. miCLIP-seq m6A sites) and isoform-derived regions such as stop codons, and you need a TxIA-aware weighted overlap statistic rather than a raw genomic overlap count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/weighted-overlap-colocalization-measure))
- **weighted-partitions-interval-restrictions**: Exact formulas and bivariate master identity methodology for weighted partitions with interval restrictions. Use when: deriving generating functions for restricted partition functions, proving partition coefficient bounds via Rogers-Fine evaluation, establishing master identities with auxiliary variables, or analyzing quantum modular forms from interval-restricted partitions. Activation: weighted partitions, interval restrictions, bivariate master identity, Rogers-Fine evaluation, false theta partition, Andrews El Bachraoui, partition coefficient bounds, quantum modular partition ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/weighted-partitions-interval-restrictions) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **weighted-pathway-similarity-scoring**: Use when two KEGG organisms' metabolic networks have been reconstructed at the structural and functional levels and you need quantitative global similarity scores (Psim, PsimW, Csim) per organism pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-pathway-similarity-scoring))
- **weighted-penalty-score-match-selection**: Use when two post-QC LC–MS feature tables (RT, m/z, FI medians) have been candidate-matched but some features remain in clusters with multiple candidate matches and need unique one-to-one correspondence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/weighted-penalty-score-match-selection))

([ryanthedev/code-foundations](https://github.com/ryanthedev/code-foundations/tree/HEAD/skills/welc-legacy-code) · ★390)

Not for greenfield TDD or code already well covered. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-legacy-code-change-algorithm))
- **welc-property-based-design-pressure**: Use when property-based tests are hard to write, properties keep needing exception clauses, invariants are hard to state universally, or when choosing between example-based and property-based testing. Treat that difficulty as design feedback. Not for library or API usage questions. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-property-based-design-pressure))
- **welc-scratch-refactoring**: Use when reading a large, tangled legacy module has not produced a working mental model: refactor aggressively on a throwaway branch (extract, rename, reorganize) purely to understand the control flow and find seams, then discard the branch before making the real change. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-scratch-refactoring))
- **welc-sprout-wrap-decision**: Use when adding new behavior to existing code that cannot be tested right away and choosing among Sprout Method, Sprout Class, Wrap Method, or Wrap Class. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-sprout-wrap-decision))
- **welc-tended-untended-systems**: Use when deciding how much testing to invest in a system based on its deployment model: tended (hotfixable, like cloud services) vs untended (firmware, embedded, regulated) and transient vs long-lived code, or when comparing test practices across those system types. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-tended-untended-systems))
- **welc-three-goals-of-testing**: Use when tests are not helping: high coverage but bugs still ship, a slow suite nobody trusts, TDD not catching regressions, refactoring breaking tests, or choosing between unit, integration, and acceptance tests. Covers the purpose and mix of a test suite. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/testing-legacy/skills/welc-three-goals-of-testing))
- **welch-oneway-anova-metabolite-features**: Use when a preprocessed MetaboSet peak table from MS-DIAL via notame must be tested feature-wise across two or more study groups, and per-feature p-values need multiple-testing correction. - **welch-t-test-asynchrony-comparison**: Use when comparing whether peak-gene pairs ranked by a Granger-causal method show significantly longer chromatin-to-expression pseudotime lags than pairs ranked by Pearson or pseudocell correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-asynchrony-comparison))
- **welch-t-test-cell-proportion-testing**: Use when PILOT's Wasserstein-distance-based Leiden clustering has assigned patient samples to clusters (e.g., the PDAC 11 control / 14 Tumor 1 / 10 Tumor 2 split) and you need to test which cell-type proportions differ between clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-cell-proportion-testing))
- **welch-t-test-de-testing**: Use when a clustered single-cell RNA-seq AnnData object needs a per-cluster DE profile to assign a cell type, e.g. testing one Leiden cluster against the rest before GO/KEGG/MSigDB enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-de-testing))
- **welch-t-test-differential-expression**: Use when each cell in a single-cell dataset (scRNA-seq or a scATAC-seq cell-by-gene activity matrix) already has cluster labels and you must identify marker genes per cluster via one-vs-rest testing, ranked by fold-change for marker or enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-t-test-differential-expression))
- **welch-t-test-log-transformed-intensities**: Use when comparing log-transformed GC-TOF MS metabolite intensity distributions between two groups of biological metadata triads (organ/species/disease) and you need per-compound p-values with unequal-variance robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-t-test-log-transformed-intensities))
- **welch-t-test-statistical-comparison**: Use when two independent groups of per-pair scores (e.g., APSS from SNP-only vs indel-only Bacmeta simulations) must be compared statistically, and per-point variances or sample sizes may be unequal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/welch-t-test-statistical-comparison))
- **welch-test-log-transformed-intensity-comparison**: Use when comparing metabolite intensities across two broad biological ontology categories (e.g. 'digestive tract' vs 'Bacteria') over many metadata-combination pairs, where unequal variances and log-normal intensity distributions require Welch's t-test on log-transformed values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-test-log-transformed-intensity-comparison))
- **welch-two-sample-t-test-metabolomics**: Use when raw per-metabolite p-values are needed from a two-group comparison (e.g., sex) of ungrouped log-transformed metabolite abundances via Welch's unequal-variance t-tests, prior to eigen-analysis-based FWER adjustment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-two-sample-t-test-metabolomics))
- **welch-two-sample-t-testing**: Use when cells have been assigned intact or mutant gRNAs by GiRAFR and you need to test whether mutant gRNA cells fail to downregulate their target genes relative to intact gRNA cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/welch-two-sample-t-testing))
- **welch-two-sample-ttest-adduct-abundance-comparison**: Use when validating ion-identity/adduct assignments from an aligned MS1 feature table across experimental conditions where a specific adduct class should change in abundance (e.g. post-column salt infusion of sodium acetate or ammonium acetate vs H2O control). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/welch-two-sample-ttest-adduct-abundance-comparison))
- **welchs-t-test-significance-testing**: Use when a putative DEL/DUP call (e.g., from Sniffles/CuteSV/SVIM/NanoVar nanopore pipelines) needs coverage-based validation: supported if coverage shift &gt;=40% with Welch t-test p &lt; 0.05, discarded otherwise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/welchs-t-test-significance-testing))

- **wenxuan-learn**: 选题深挖学习引擎；当用户使用 /wenxuan-learn、想写某个选题、要求调研或深挖选题、要求系统性研究一个产品/公司/技术/人物/概念/文章内容时，搜索并生成研究报告（强制事实核查，剔除不实数据），统一 10000-20000 字 + 4 章模板，推动用户输出后衔接 wenxuan-writer。v2.0 核心：双轴分析法（纵轴追时间深度+横轴追同期广度+交汇出判断）作为**默认骨架**（任何主题任何事情都走双轴 + 正反竞奇，打通不分离），5 类研究对象适配（含文章/内容拆解），A/B/C 竞品三场景，决策逻辑强制还原，横纵交汇 5 必答 + 三剧本推演，14 条质检清单，必选 PDF 输出（scripts/md_to_pdf.py）。v1.1 既有能力：并行子 agent 提速、选题方向锁防越界、写作后验反馈环闭环。 ([huangwenxuangod/wenxuan-skills](https://github.com/huangwenxuangod/wenxuan-skills/tree/HEAD/wenxuan-learn) · ★2)
